\displaystyle(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)-(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+\left((\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)-(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)+\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{v}r}\right)E_{1}-\left(\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{u}r}\right)E_{4},

and

(5.157)

\displaystyle(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)-(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+\left((\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)-(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)+\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{v}r}\right)E_{1}-\left(\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{u}r}\right)E_{4}.

By substituting (
5.156
) into the expression for
\partial_{u}\tilde{f}
in (
5.146
), we obtain

(5.158)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\tilde{f}}{\partial u}=

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\left(1+\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)+4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)

\displaystyle+\re\left(4\pi\left(\frac{(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{v}r}\right)E_{1}-4\pi\left(\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{u}r}\right)E_{4}\right)+E_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)+4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)+E_{5}

in which

(5.159)

E_{5}:=\left(4\pi\left(\frac{(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{v}r}\right)E_{1}-4\pi\left(\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\partial_{u}r}\right)E_{4}\right)+E_{3}.

Note that since
|E_{1}|\lesssim Dr
,
|E_{3}|\lesssim D^{2}
,
|E_{4}|\lesssim Dr
,
|r\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|\lesssim D
,
|r\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|\lesssim D
, we can deduce that
|E_{5}|\lesssim D^{2}
.

By a parallel argument, for
\partial_{v}\tilde{f}^{*}
, we can utilize (
5.157
) to (
5.148
) to deduce that

(5.160)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\tilde{f}^{*}}{\partial v}=

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)+4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)+E_{5}.

Comparing terms in the expressions for
\frac{\partial\tilde{f}}{\partial u}
and for
\frac{\partial\tilde{f}^{*}}{\partial v}
above with that for
\partial_{v}\partial_{u}\log\lambda
and for
\partial_{u}\partial_{v}\log\left(-\nu\right)
in (
5.139
) and (
5.140
), it is instructional to consider following decomposition of the term
|D_{u}\phi|^{2}
:

(5.161)

\displaystyle|D_{u}\phi|^{2}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{u}\phi+\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\phi)(\partial_{u}\phi-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\phi)^{\dagger}=|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|^{2}-2\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\im(\phi\partial_{u}\phi^{\dagger})+\mathfrak{e}^{2}A_{u}^{2}|\phi|^{2}.

This implies that (
5.139
) and (
5.140
) can be simplified as

(5.162)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\partial_{u}\log\lambda=

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}\right)+E_{6}

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)

and

(5.163)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\partial_{v}\log(-\nu)=

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{u}r}{r}\right)+E_{6}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{(r|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{v}r)}{\partial_{u}r}-\frac{(r|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|)^{2}(\partial_{u}r)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)

with

(5.164)

\displaystyle E_{6}:=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{1}{1-\mu}\right)\left((\partial_{u}r)\im(\phi^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\phi)+(\partial_{v}r)\im(\phi^{\dagger}D_{u}\phi)\right)

\displaystyle+4\pi\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{v}r}{\partial_{u}r}\right)\left(2\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\im(\phi\partial_{u}\phi^{\dagger})-\mathfrak{e}^{2}A_{u}^{2}|\phi|^{2}\right)

satisfying
|E_{6}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}
. Substituting the expressions of
\frac{\partial\tilde{f}}{\partial u}
and
\frac{\partial\tilde{f}^{*}}{\partial v}
in (
5.158
) and in (
5.160
) to (
5.162
) and to (
5.163
), we obtain

(5.165)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\partial_{v}\log\lambda\right)=\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}-\tilde{f}\right)+4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)+E_{5}+E_{6}

and

(5.166)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\partial_{u}\log(-\nu)\right)=\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{u}r}{r}+\tilde{f}^{*}\right)-4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)-E_{5}+E_{6}.

With the above preparations, we start to estimate the total variations of
\log(\lambda),\log(-\nu)
and
\log(\lambda|_{\Gamma})
. Employing (
5.165
), for a fixed
(u,v)\in\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})
, we first integrate along a future-directed incoming null curve. Together with the fact that
\partial_{v}\lambda(0,v)=0
, we obtain

(5.167)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\log\lambda(u,v)=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}-\tilde{f}\right)(u,v)-\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}-\tilde{f}\right)(0,v)

\displaystyle+4\pi\int^{u}_{0}\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}+\int^{u}_{0}(E_{5}+E_{6})(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

Further integrating (
5.167
) for
v\in(u,v_{0})
, we derive

(5.168)

\displaystyle\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\left|\partial_{v}\log\lambda-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}+\tilde{f}\right|(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\left|\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}-\tilde{f}\right|(0,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+D^{2}

To obtain the total variation of
\lambda
, it suffices to estimate the integral in the upper bound of (
5.168
). To estimate the term
\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}
, we appeal to (
1.21
) and see that

(5.169)

\partial_{v}\log\left(\frac{-\partial_{u}r}{1-\mu}\right)=\frac{4\pi r|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}}{\partial_{v}r}.

By (
5.136
), we can rewrite this as

(5.170)

\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}-\partial_{v}\log\left(1-\mu\right)=\frac{4\pi r|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}}{\partial_{v}r}.

Integrating both sides of (
5.170
) with respect to
v^{\prime}
, we obtain

(5.171)

\displaystyle\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{(\partial_{v}r)}{r}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\log(1-\mu(u,v_{0}))+4\pi\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\frac{r|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}}{\partial_{v}r}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim D\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\partial_{v}\phi|(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\lesssim DX

and

(5.172)

\displaystyle\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{(\partial_{v}r)}{r}(0,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\log(1-\mu(u,v_{0}))+4\pi\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\frac{r|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}}{\partial_{v}r}(0,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim D\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\partial_{v}\phi|(0,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\lesssim D\cdot TV_{\{0\}\times(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\alpha}]

\displaystyle\lesssim D\cdot\left(2\cdot TV_{\{0\}\times(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}]\right)\lesssim D^{2}.

In the above computations, we have used
\mu|_{\Gamma}=0
,
\mu<1
,
\frac{r|\partial_{v}\phi|}{\partial_{v}r}\lesssim D
,
\lambda(0,v)=\frac{1}{2}
and Lemma
5.2
. Furthermore, from the definition of
\tilde{f}
in (
5.141
), we have

(5.173)

|\tilde{f}|(u,v)\lesssim D|\partial_{v}\phi|(u,v)

and hence

(5.174)

\displaystyle\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\tilde{f}|(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim D\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\partial_{v}\phi|(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\lesssim DX,

\displaystyle\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\tilde{f}|(0,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim D\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\partial_{v}\phi|(0,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\lesssim D^{2}.

Gathering the estimates in (
5.171
), (
5.172
), (
5.174
), by (
5.168
), we conclude that

(5.175)

\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\log\lambda]\lesssim D^{2}+DX+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu].

Next, we move on to estimate the total variation of
\log(-\nu)
. Observe that from (
5.75
), (
5.76
), and (
5.142
) that we have

(5.176)

|\tilde{f}^{*}|(u,v)\lesssim r(u,v).

Together with (
5.79
), this implies

(5.177)

\tilde{f}^{*}|_{\Gamma}=\frac{\mu}{r}|_{\Gamma}=0.

Using a symmetric argument as for (
5.166
), by (
5.114
), we get

(5.178)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\log(-\nu)(u,v)=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}\log(-\nu)(u)+\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{u}r}{r}+4\pi\tilde{f}^{*}\right)(u,v)

\displaystyle-4\pi\int^{v}_{u}\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}+\int^{v}_{u}(E_{6}-E_{5})(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

and hence

(5.179)

\displaystyle\int^{v}_{0}\left|\partial_{u}\log(-\nu)-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{u}r}{r}-4\pi\tilde{f}^{*}\right|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\log(-\nu)|_{\Gamma}]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}.

Similar to the arguments made above, we start from (
1.20
) and rewrite (
5.134
) as

(5.180)

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{u}r}{r}-\partial_{u}\log(1-\mu)

\displaystyle=\frac{4\pi r|D_{u}\phi|^{2}}{\partial_{u}r}.

Integrating with respect to
u^{\prime}
, recalling the definition of
Y
in (
5.11
) and applying
|D_{u}\phi|=|\partial_{u}\phi+\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}r\phi|\lesssim|\partial_{u}\phi|+D
, we deduce that

(5.181)

\displaystyle\int^{v}_{0}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{(-\partial_{u}r)}{r}(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim D\int^{v}_{u}|D_{u}\phi|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim D\int^{v}_{u}|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}+D^{2}\lesssim DY+D^{2}.

In addition, from the expression of
\tilde{f}^{*}
in (
5.142
), we see that

(5.182)

|\tilde{f}^{*}|(u,v)\lesssim D|\partial_{u}\phi|(u,v)

and thus obtain

(5.183)

\displaystyle\int^{v}_{0}|\tilde{f}^{*}|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim D\cdot T.V_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim DY.

By applying (
5.113
), (
5.181
), (
5.183
) to (
5.179
), we hence derive

(5.184)

\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\log|\nu|]\lesssim D^{2}+DY+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\log\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda].

To estimate
TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\log\lambda|_{\Gamma}]
, we appeal to (
5.127
), (
5.165
), (
5.177
). Integrating along future-directed incoming null curve from
C_{0}^{+}
to
(u,u)\in\Gamma
yields

(5.185)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}\log\left(\lambda|_{\Gamma}\right)(u)=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\partial_{v}r}{r}-4\pi\tilde{f}\right)(0,u)

\displaystyle+4\pi\int^{u}_{0}\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)(u^{\prime},u)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}+\int^{u}_{0}(E_{5}+E_{6})(u^{\prime},u)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

By utilizing estimates analogous to (
5.172
) and (
5.174
), we deduce that

(5.186)

TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\log(\lambda|_{\Gamma})]\lesssim D^{2}+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu].

As the next course of action, we proceed to establish relations between the different area terms that have appeared in the estimates above. Starting from
A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]
, by (
5.40
), we observe that

(5.187)

\partial_{u}\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}=\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\partial_{v}r+E_{7}\quad\text{ with }\quad E_{7}:=-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}\left(A_{u}\partial_{v}(r\tilde{\phi})+\phi_{0}A_{u}\partial_{v}r+\frac{\partial_{u}r\partial_{v}r}{1-\mu}\frac{Q\phi}{r}\right)

and
|E_{7}|(u,v)\lesssim Dr(u,v)
. This then implies that

(5.188)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle=\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)-\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}(\partial_{v}\tilde{\mathfrak{a}})-(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}(\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\partial_{v}r+E_{7})}{\lambda}+\frac{(r\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})+\tilde{\phi}\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\lambda}(\partial_{v}\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{v}\lambda)+\frac{(r\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}-\frac{(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}E_{7}}{\lambda}.

Furthermore, applying (
5.84
), (
5.87
), (
5.102
), (
5.103
), we get

(5.189)

\left|\partial_{v}\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{v}\lambda\right|(u,v)\leq F(v)

with

(5.190)

F(v)=\left|\partial_{v}\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{v}\lambda\right|(0,v)+\int^{u}_{0}\left|G_{1}+G_{2}+\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

and

(5.191)

\int^{v_{0}}_{0}F(v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim D\left(1+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]\right)+A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}].

Note that in (
5.191
), we have used
A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]=A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]
. This follows from the fact that, for any complex number
z
, we have
|z|=|z^{\dagger}|
. Back to (
5.189
), we then derive

(5.192)

\displaystyle\int_{D(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|}{\lambda}|\partial_{v}\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{v}\lambda|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}\int^{v}_{0}|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\right)\left(\int^{v_{0}}_{0}F(v)\mathrm{d}v\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim DY\left(1+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]\right)+Y\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}].

Therefore, equation (
5.188
) allows us to deduce that

(5.193)

A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]\lesssim DY\left(1+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]\right)+D^{2}+(D+Y)\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}].

We conduct a similar strategy to control
A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]
as follows. Observe that from (
5.105
), we have

(5.194)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\partial_{v}r+E_{8}\quad\text{ with }\quad E_{8}:=\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}\frac{\partial_{u}r\partial_{v}r}{1-\mu}\frac{Q\phi}{r}

and
|E_{8}|(u,v)\lesssim Dr(u,v)
. Together with (
5.104
), we compute
\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}
and obtain

(5.195)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)-(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}(\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\partial_{v}r+E_{8})-(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}(\partial_{u}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}})}{\nu}+\frac{(r\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})+\tilde{\phi}\nu+\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}r\phi}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}}{\nu}\left(\partial_{u}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\nu\right)+\frac{(r\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})+\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}r\phi}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi})^{\dagger}E_{8}}{\nu}.

Employing (
5.109
), (
5.123
), (
5.119
), (
5.122
), we see that

(5.196)

\left|\partial_{u}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\nu\right|(u,v)\leq F^{*}(u)

with

(5.197)

F^{*}(u)=\left|\partial_{u}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\nu\right|_{\Gamma}(u)+\int^{v}_{u}\left|G_{3}+G_{4}-\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

and

(5.198)

\int_{0}^{v_{0}}F^{*}(u)\mathrm{d}u\lesssim TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}|_{\Gamma}]+D\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)+A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}].

Note that in the expression of (
5.197
), the definitions of
G_{3}
and
G_{4}
were given (
5.108
). Consequently, we deduce that

(5.199)

\displaystyle\int_{D(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|}{|\nu|}|\partial_{u}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}-\tilde{\phi}\partial_{u}\nu|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\left(\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}\int^{v_{0}}_{u}|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|(u,v)\mathrm{d}v\right)\left(\int^{v_{0}}_{0}F^{*}(u)\mathrm{d}u\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle X\cdot TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}|_{\Gamma}]+DX\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)+X\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}].

Combining (
5.195
) and (
5.199
), we now arrive at the following estimate

(5.200)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]\lesssim

\displaystyle X\cdot TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}|_{\Gamma}]+DX\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)

\displaystyle+D^{2}+(D+X)\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}].

In view of (
5.193
) and (
5.200
), we proceed to estimate
A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]
and
A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]
. For convenience, we denote

(5.201)

\tilde{\theta}:=r\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}=r\partial_{v}\phi,\quad\quad\tilde{\zeta}:=r\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}=r\partial_{u}\phi,\quad\quad\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}:=rD_{u}\phi.

It is worth noting that, contrary to (
5.201
), here
\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}\neq rD_{u}\tilde{\phi}
but rather
\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}=\tilde{\zeta}+\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}r\phi.

Inspired by the definition of
\tilde{f}
in (
5.141
), we also define

(5.202)

\tilde{g}:=4\pi\im\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right).

By (
5.145
), we have

(5.203)

\tilde{f}+\mathrm{i}\tilde{g}=4\pi\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu}+E_{2}\right)

with
E_{2}
given in (
5.145
). This further implies that

(5.204)

-\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda\nu}(\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta})=-\frac{\tilde{f}\tilde{\zeta}}{r}-\mathrm{i}\frac{\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{g}}{r}+\frac{4\pi\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}E_{2}.

Together with (
5.134
), we now compute
\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}
as follows

(5.205)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\log\lambda}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\tilde{\phi}}{\partial v}-\frac{\partial\log\lambda}{\partial v}\frac{\partial\tilde{\phi}}{\partial u}=\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\nu\tilde{\theta}}{r^{2}}-\partial_{v}\log\lambda\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}(\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta})\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}+4\pi\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\zeta}}{\lambda\nu}\right)-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{r}\left(\partial_{v}\log\lambda-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\lambda}{r}\right)-\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\zeta}}{\lambda\nu}(\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}}{\lambda\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}\right)-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{r}\left(\partial_{v}\log\lambda-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\lambda}{r}+\tilde{f}\right)

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\frac{\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{g}}{r}+\frac{4\pi\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}E_{2}-4\pi\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}\frac{\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}}{\lambda\nu}\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}A_{u}\phi}{r}.

By recalling and re-writing (
5.165
), we have

(5.206)

\partial_{u}\left(\partial_{v}\log\lambda-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\lambda}{r}+\tilde{f}\right)=4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)+E_{5}+E_{6},

with
|E_{5}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}
and
|E_{6}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}
. Henceforth, we obtain

(5.207)

\left|\partial_{v}\log\lambda-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\lambda}{r}+\tilde{f}\right|(u,v)\leq G(v)

with

(5.208)

G(v):=\left|\partial_{v}\log\lambda-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\lambda}{r}+\tilde{f}\right|(0,v)+4\pi\int^{v}_{0}\left|\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{v}|E_{5}|+|E_{6}|(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

Together with (
5.172
) and (
5.174
), we have

(5.209)

\int_{0}^{v_{0}}G(v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim D^{2}+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu].

Next, we define

(5.210)

\tilde{\rho}:=r\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}\right).

Back to (
5.205
), with
|E_{2}|(u,v)\lesssim Dr(u,v)^{2}
, we now deduce that

(5.211)

\left|\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|\lesssim D\frac{|\tilde{\rho}|}{r^{3}}+|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|G(v)+D\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}|}{r^{3}}+D^{3}.

Integrating on the domain
\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})
, we then have

(5.212)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{\partial(\lambda,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\lesssim

\displaystyle D\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|(u,v)G(v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle+\;D\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v+D^{3}.

By (
5.11
), we note that

(5.213)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}|(u,v)G(v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim Y\int^{v_{0}}_{0}G(v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim Y(D^{2}+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]).

Meanwhile, by (
5.69
), (
5.72
), (
5.74
), we have

(5.214)

|\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}|(u,v)\lesssim r(u,v),\quad|\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}|(u,v)\lesssim r(u,v)+Dr^{3}(u,v)\lesssim r(u,v).

Together with (
5.79
) and
0\leq\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}\leq\mu
, we prove

(5.215)

|\tilde{\rho}|(u,v)\lesssim r^{3}(u,v).

Thus, we deduce that

(5.216)

\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{r^{2}}|_{\Gamma}=0.

Similarly, one can also deduce that
|\tilde{g}|(u,v)\lesssim r(u,v)
and hence it implies

(5.217)

\tilde{g}|_{\Gamma}=0.

Using (
5.216
) and conducting integration by parts, we obtain

(5.218)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int^{v_{0}}_{0}\left(\int^{v}_{0}|\tilde{\rho}/\nu|\partial_{u}\left(\frac{1}{r^{2}}\right)(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\right)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int^{v_{0}}_{0}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}/\nu|}{r^{2}}(0,v)\mathrm{d}v-\frac{1}{2}\int^{v_{0}}_{0}\left(\int^{v}_{0}\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{u}\left|\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\nu}\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\right)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\nu}\right)\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v.

In a similar fashion, by (
5.217
), we also get

(5.219)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{u}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}}{\nu}\right)\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v.

It remains to compute
\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\nu}\right)
and
\partial_{u}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}}{\nu}\right)
. For the former, we have

(5.220)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{r}{\nu}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}+r\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda+4\pi\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}+r\lambda\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)+\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}r\partial_{u}\lambda

\displaystyle+4\pi r\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{\nu}\right)(D_{u}\tilde{\theta}-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\tilde{\theta})^{\dagger}+4\pi r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{\nu}\right).

Subsequently, observe that from (
1.27
) and (
1.30
), we can write the term
\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)
as

(5.221)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)=

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)=\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{(1-\mu)^{2}}\partial_{u}\mu+\frac{1}{1-\mu}\partial_{u}\left(-\frac{Q^{2}}{r^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{(1-\mu)^{2}}\left(\frac{4\pi(1-\mu)|\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}|^{2}}{r\nu}-\frac{\nu}{r}(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})\right)-\frac{1}{1-\mu}\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\im(\phi^{\dagger}D_{u}\phi)-\frac{2Q^{2}\nu}{r^{3}}\right).

Furthermore, observe that from (
1.24
), we can express the term
D_{u}\tilde{\theta}
as

(5.222)

D_{u}\tilde{\theta}=D_{u}(r\partial_{v}\phi)=-\lambda\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{r}-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}\frac{Q\phi\lambda\nu}{r(1-\mu)}.

In addition, from (
5.104
), we have

(5.223)

\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\nu\tilde{\phi}+rD_{u}\phi}{\nu}\right)=\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{r}-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\tilde{\phi}+\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{\nu}\right).

Collecting (
1.19
) and (
5.221
) - (
5.223
), we can reorganize and simplify (
5.220
) as

(5.224)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}+\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)^{2}\lambda\nu

\displaystyle+r\lambda\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{(1-\mu)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{4\pi(1-\mu)|\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}|^{2}}{r\nu}-\frac{\nu}{r}(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})\right)-\frac{r\lambda}{1-\mu}\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\im(\phi^{\dagger}D_{u}\phi)-\frac{2Q^{2}\nu}{r^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle+4\pi r\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{\nu}\right)\left(-\lambda\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{r}-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}\frac{Q\phi\lambda\nu}{r(1-\mu)}-\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\tilde{\theta}\right)^{\dagger}+4\pi r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\left(\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{r}+\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}\tilde{\phi}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi\lambda(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})|\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}|^{2}}{\nu(1-\mu)}+4\pi r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+E_{9}

with

(5.225)

E_{9}:=-\frac{r\lambda}{1-\mu}\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\im(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}D_{u}\phi)-\frac{2Q^{2}\nu}{r^{3}}\right)+4\pi\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}r\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}}{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{Q\phi\lambda\nu}{r(1-\mu)}+A_{u}\tilde{\theta}\right)^{\dagger}+4\pi\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}A_{u}\tilde{\phi}.

Note that in the process of arriving at the last line in (
5.224
), numerous cancellations happen.
On top of that, since
|\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}|\lesssim D
, we then have
|E_{9}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}r^{2}(u,v).
Moreover, with the help of (
5.152
) and (
5.161
), we can also write

(5.226)

\displaystyle\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle r\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}=r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r}\left(-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu}\frac{\lambda}{r}+\frac{E_{4}}{\nu}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}\right)-\frac{\lambda(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{r\nu(1-\mu)}+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\nu}E_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}\right)-\frac{\lambda(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})|\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\zeta}$}}|^{2}}{r\nu(1-\mu)}+E_{10}

with

(5.227)

E_{10}:=-\frac{\lambda(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})}{r\nu(1-\mu)}(2\mathfrak{e}r^{2}A_{u}\im(\phi\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})-r^{2}\mathfrak{e}^{2}A_{u}^{2}|\phi|^{2})+\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\nu}E_{4}

and
|E_{10}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}r(u,v)
since
|E_{4}|(u,v)\lesssim Dr(u,v)
. Combining (
5.224
) and (
5.226
), we can now write

(5.228)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=4\pi r^{2}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+E_{11}

with

(5.229)

E_{11}:=E_{9}+4\pi rE_{10}

and
|E_{11}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}r^{2}(u,v)
. Applying (
5.218
), this implies that

(5.230)

\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\lesssim A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+D^{2}.

Next, we continue to estimate
\partial_{u}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}}{\nu}\right)
and we first compute

(5.231)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}r}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}+r\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{\nu}\right)\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+\frac{r}{\nu}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right).

Using (
5.143
), we can rewrite
\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{\nu}\right)
as

(5.232)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\nu\partial_{u}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\nu}{\nu^{2}}=-\frac{\lambda}{r\nu}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}+\frac{E_{1}^{\dagger}}{\nu}-\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\nu}{\nu^{2}}.

Together with (
5.156
), we then simplify (
5.231
) to

(5.233)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}r}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)=

\displaystyle\left(\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\frac{\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\nu}+\frac{rE_{1}^{\dagger}}{\nu}-\frac{r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\nu}{\nu^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{r^{2}}{\nu}\left(\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\lambda}{\nu}-\frac{|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}\nu}{\lambda}\right)+\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}E_{4}}{r\nu}\right).

Appealing to (
5.145
), we have

(5.234)

\displaystyle\left(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\frac{r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\nu}{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle\left(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\frac{r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\nu}{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu}+E_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}-(\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\zeta}+\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta})+\frac{\lambda}{\nu}|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}+E_{2}(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger})

\displaystyle-\frac{r}{\nu\lambda}\partial_{u}\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}+(\partial_{u}\nu)\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\left(\frac{r}{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu}-E_{2}\right).

On the other hand, from (
1.19
), we have

(5.235)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=r(\partial_{u}\nu\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})=r\left(\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\partial_{v}\nu\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\right)=r\left(\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\lambda\nu\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right).

Hence, we obtain

(5.236)

\displaystyle\left(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\frac{r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\nu}{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}-(\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\zeta}+\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta})+\frac{\lambda}{\nu}|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}-\frac{r}{\nu\lambda}\partial_{u}\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}+\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}

\displaystyle+\frac{r^{2}\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+E_{2}(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger})-E_{2}\left(\frac{r^{2}}{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\lambda\nu\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right).

Plugging this back to (
5.233
), we arrive at

(5.237)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}r}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu}\left(\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}-(\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\zeta}+\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta})+\frac{\lambda}{\nu}|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}-\frac{r}{\nu\lambda}\partial_{u}\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}+\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)+\frac{r^{2}\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu^{3}}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle+\frac{E_{2}}{\nu}(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger})-E_{2}\left(\frac{r^{2}}{\nu^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\lambda\nu\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)+\frac{rE_{1}^{\dagger}}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\nu}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\lambda}{\nu}-\frac{|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}\nu}{\lambda}\right)+\frac{r^{2}}{\nu}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{r^{2}}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}E_{4}}{r\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle r^{2}\frac{(\tilde{\zeta}/\nu-E_{2})}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+r^{2}\frac{1}{\nu}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}+R_{1}+E_{12},

with

(5.238)

\displaystyle R_{1}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu}\left(\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}-(\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\zeta}+\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta})+\frac{\lambda}{\nu}|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}-\frac{r}{\nu\lambda}\partial_{u}\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}+\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\nu}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\lambda}{\nu}-\frac{|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}\nu}{\lambda}\right),

\displaystyle E_{12}:=

\displaystyle\frac{E_{2}}{\nu}(\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger})-\frac{E_{2}\nu\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}+\frac{rE_{1}^{\dagger}}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+\frac{r^{2}}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}E_{4}}{r\nu}\right).

Note that to arrive at the last equality in (
5.237
), numerous cancellations were explored. By recognizing that
\im(R_{1})=0
and
|E_{12}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}r^{2}(u,v)
, we can take the imaginary part of (
5.237
) and deduce that

(5.239)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=4\pi\im\left(\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}r}{\nu}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=4\pi\im\left(r^{2}\frac{(\tilde{\zeta}/\nu-E_{2})}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}+r^{2}\frac{1}{\nu}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}+E_{12}\right).

This implies

(5.240)

\left|\partial_{u}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}}{\nu}\right)\right|\lesssim r^{2}\left(D\left|\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|+\left|\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu)}{\partial(u,v)}\right|+D^{2}\right).

Plugging (
5.240
) back to (
5.219
), we thus derive

(5.241)

\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\lesssim D\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+D^{2}.

Henceforth, by utilizing (
5.212
), (
5.213
), (
5.230
), (
5.241
) and the fact that
A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]=A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]
, we arrive at

(5.242)

A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]\lesssim(D+Y)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+D^{2}\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]+D^{2}Y+D^{3}.

It remains to estimate
A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]
. Parallel to the above arguments, we start off by defining

(5.243)

\tilde{g}^{*}:=4\pi\im\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)

and we have

(5.244)

\tilde{f}^{*}+\mathrm{i}\tilde{g}^{*}=4\pi\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu}+E_{2}\right)

with
E_{2}
given in (
5.145
). This implies that

(5.245)

\frac{4\pi}{r^{2}}\frac{\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda\nu}\left(\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}\right)=\frac{\tilde{f}^{*}\tilde{\theta}}{r}+\mathrm{i}\frac{\tilde{g}^{*}\tilde{\theta}}{r}-\frac{4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}E_{2}.

Together with (
5.136
), we compute

(5.246)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}=\partial_{u}\log|\nu|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}-\partial_{v}\log|\nu|\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}=\partial_{u}\log|\nu|\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{r}-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\lambda\tilde{\zeta}}{r^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{r^{2}}(\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta})\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}+4\pi\frac{\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda\nu}\right)+\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{r}\left(\partial_{u}\log|\nu|-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\nu}{r}\right)-4\pi\frac{\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}(\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{\nu\tilde{\theta}-\lambda\tilde{\zeta}}{\lambda\nu}\right)\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\right)+\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{r}\left(\partial_{u}\log|\nu|-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\nu}{r}-\tilde{f}^{*}\right)-\mathrm{i}\frac{\tilde{\theta}\tilde{g}^{*}}{r}+4\pi\frac{\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}E_{2}.

Note that from (
5.166
), we have

(5.247)

\partial_{v}\left(\partial_{u}\log|\nu|-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\nu}{r}-\tilde{f}^{*}\right)=-4\pi\re\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right)-E_{5}+E_{6}.

This implies that

(5.248)

\left|\partial_{u}\log|\nu|-\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\nu}{r}-\tilde{f}^{*}\right|(u,v)\leq G^{*}(u)

with

(5.249)

G^{*}(u):=\left|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}\log\left|\nu|_{\Gamma}\right|\right|(u)+4\pi\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\left|\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}v+\int_{u}^{v_{0}}|E_{5}|+|E_{6}|(u,v)\mathrm{d}v.

In (
5.249
), we have used the fact that both
\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\frac{\nu}{r}
and
\tilde{f}^{*}
vanishes at
\Gamma
.
By (
5.116
) and (
5.249
), we then deduce that

(5.250)

\int_{0}^{v_{0}}G^{*}(u)\mathrm{d}u\lesssim TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}.

Parallel to (
5.210
), we set

(5.251)

\tilde{\rho}^{*}:=r\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\right).

Back to (
5.246
), we now obtain

(5.252)

\left|\frac{1}{\nu}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|\lesssim D\frac{|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|}{r^{3}}+|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|G^{*}(u)+D\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}|}{r^{3}}+D^{3}.

Integrating on the domain
\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})
, we hence derive

(5.253)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{\nu}\frac{\partial(\nu,\tilde{\phi})}{\partial(u,v)}\right|\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim D\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v+\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|(u,v)G^{*}(u)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle+D\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v+D^{3}.

We then estimate the integrals in the right of (
5.253
). First, note that by (
5.11
), we have

(5.254)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}|\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}|(u,v)G^{*}(u)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim X\int_{0}^{v_{0}}G^{*}(u)\mathrm{d}u\lesssim X(TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}).

On the other hand, analogously to the above, one can show that

(5.255)

|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|(u,v)\lesssim r^{3}(u,v)\quad\text{ and }\quad|\tilde{g}^{*}|(u,v)\lesssim r(u,v)

and thus

(5.256)

\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{r^{2}}|_{\Gamma}=0\quad\text{ and }\quad\tilde{g}^{*}|_{\Gamma}=0.

By employing (
5.256
), we perform integration by parts and obtain

(5.257)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int^{v_{0}}_{0}\left(\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|}{-\lambda}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{1}{r^{2}}\right)(u,v)\mathrm{d}v\right)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int^{v_{0}}_{0}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|/\lambda}{r^{2}}(u,v_{0})\mathrm{d}u+\frac{1}{2}\int^{v_{0}}_{0}\int^{v_{0}}_{u}\partial_{v}\left|\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{\lambda}\right|\cdot\frac{1}{r^{2}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v.

Meanwhile, a symmetric argument with (
5.256
) yields

(5.258)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{v}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)\right|(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v.

It remains to compute
\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)
and
\partial_{v}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)
. For the former, analogously to (
5.220
), we have

(5.259)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)=

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{r}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}+r\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\nu+\frac{4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}+r\nu\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)+\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)r\partial_{v}\nu

\displaystyle+4\pi r\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}\right)\partial_{v}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}+4\pi r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}\right).

By (
1.28
) and (
1.29
), we can write
\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)
as

(5.260)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{(1-\mu)^{2}}\partial_{v}\mu+\frac{1}{1-\mu}\partial_{v}\left(-\frac{Q^{2}}{r^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{(1-\mu)^{2}}\left(\frac{4\pi(1-\mu)|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\lambda}{r}(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})\right)+\frac{1}{1-\mu}\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\im(\phi^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})+\frac{2Q^{2}\lambda}{r^{3}}\right).

In addition, to treat the last term in (
5.259
), we observe that

(5.261)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)=\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\partial_{v}r\tilde{\phi}+r\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}}{\partial_{v}r}\right)=\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{r}+\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}\right).

In conjunction with (
1.19
) and (
5.143
), the above two expressions imply that (
5.259
) simplifies to

(5.262)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\lambda\nu+4\pi\tilde{\theta}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}+\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)^{2}\lambda\nu+r\nu\left(\frac{1-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{(1-\mu)^{2}}\left(\frac{4\pi(1-\mu)|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\lambda}{r}(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{r\nu}{1-\mu}\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\im(\phi^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})+\frac{2Q^{2}\lambda}{r^{3}}\right)+4\pi r\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}\right)\left(-\frac{\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r}+E_{1}^{\dagger}\right)+4\pi r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\left(\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)-\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{r}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi\nu(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda(1-\mu)}+4\pi r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)+E_{13}

with

(5.263)

E_{13}:=\frac{r\nu}{1-\mu}\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\im(\phi^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi})+\frac{2Q^{2}\lambda}{r^{3}}\right)+\frac{4\pi r\tilde{\theta}E_{1}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}

and
|E_{13}|(u,v)\lesssim D^{2}r^{2}(u,v).
Note that to arrive at the last equality in (
5.262
), various cancellations were explored. With the help of (
5.150
) and (
5.152
), we also have

(5.264)

\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)=r\left(\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\partial_{u}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=r\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}-\frac{\nu(\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2})|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{r\lambda(1-\mu)}+\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{\lambda}.

Plugging (
5.264
) into (
5.262
), we deduce that

(5.265)

\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)=4\pi r^{2}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+E_{14}\quad\text{ with }\quad E_{14}=E_{13}+\frac{4\pi r\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{\lambda}

and
|E_{14}|\lesssim D^{2}r^{2}(u,v)
. Together with (
5.257
), this implies that

(5.266)

\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|\tilde{\rho}^{*}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\lesssim A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}.

Next, we proceed to compute
\partial_{v}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}}{\nu}\right)
. First, observe that

(5.267)

\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}r}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)=\left(\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}+r\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}\right)\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+\frac{r}{\lambda}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right).

By (
5.143
), we can write

(5.268)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda\partial_{v}\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}=-\frac{\nu}{\lambda r}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}+\frac{E_{1}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}.

Combining with (
5.157
), we then simplify (
5.267
) to

(5.269)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}r}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)=

\displaystyle\left(\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\frac{\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}+\frac{rE_{1}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}-\frac{r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{r^{2}}{\lambda}\left(\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\lambda}{\nu}-\frac{|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}\nu}{\lambda}\right)+\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}E_{4}}{r\nu}\right).

Observe that by (
5.145
), we have

(5.270)

\displaystyle\left(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\frac{r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle\left(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\frac{r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}}{\nu}+E_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}+(\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta}+\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}+E_{2}(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})

\displaystyle+\frac{r|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda\nu}-(\partial_{v}\lambda)\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\left(\frac{r}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda}+E_{2}\right).

On the other hand, from (
1.19
), we have

(5.271)

\displaystyle(\partial_{v}\lambda)\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}=r((\partial_{v}\lambda)\partial_{u}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger})=r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+(\partial_{u}\lambda)\partial_{v}\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}\right)=r\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\lambda\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right),

and hence the first term in (
5.269
) has the following expression

(5.272)

\displaystyle\left(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}-\frac{r\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)=

\displaystyle-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}+(\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta}+\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}+\frac{r|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda\nu}-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}

\displaystyle-\frac{r^{2}\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+E_{2}(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-E_{2}\left(\frac{r^{2}}{\lambda}\right)\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\lambda\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right).

Plugging this back to (
5.269
), we have therefore obtained

(5.273)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}r}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}+(\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta}+\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}+\frac{r|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda\nu}-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)-\frac{r^{2}\tilde{\theta}}{\lambda^{3}}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}

\displaystyle+\frac{E_{2}}{\lambda}(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-E_{2}\left(\frac{r^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{\lambda\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{r^{2}}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)+\frac{rE_{1}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\lambda}{\nu}-\frac{|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}\nu}{\lambda}\right)+\frac{r^{2}}{\lambda}\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+\frac{r^{2}}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}E_{4}}{r\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle r^{2}\left(\frac{E_{2}-\tilde{\theta}/\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}\right)\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+r^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+R_{2}+E_{15},

with

(5.274)

\displaystyle R_{2}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}+(\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}\tilde{\theta}+\tilde{\zeta}\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-\frac{\lambda|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}}{\nu}+\frac{r|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\partial_{v}\lambda}{\lambda\nu}-\frac{\nu|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}}{\lambda}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}\right)\left(\frac{|\tilde{\zeta}|^{2}\lambda}{\nu}-\frac{|\tilde{\theta}|^{2}\nu}{\lambda}\right),

\displaystyle E_{15}:=

\displaystyle\frac{E_{2}}{\lambda}(\lambda\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}-\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger})-\frac{E_{2}\nu\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}+\frac{rE_{1}^{\dagger}}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)+\frac{r^{2}}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\theta}^{\dagger}E_{1}}{r\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}E_{4}}{r\nu}\right).

Similar to (
5.262
), to arrive at the last line in (
5.273
), multiple cancellations have to be explored.
Employing
\im(R_{2})=0
and
|E_{15}|\lesssim D^{2}r^{2}(u,v)
, we arrive at

(5.275)

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)

\displaystyle=4\pi\im\left(\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\tilde{\zeta}^{\dagger}r}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}-\frac{\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}}{\nu}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=4\pi\im\left(r^{2}\left(\frac{E_{2}-\tilde{\theta}/\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}\right)\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+r^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}+E_{15}\right)

and thus

(5.276)

\left|\partial_{v}\left(\frac{r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}}{\lambda}\right)\right|\lesssim r^{2}\left(D\left|\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right|+\left|\frac{\partial(\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda)}{\partial(u,v)}\right|+D^{2}\right).

Plugging these back to (
5.258
), we then deduce that

(5.277)

\int_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{0})}\frac{|r^{2}\tilde{g}^{*}|}{r^{3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\lesssim D\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\lambda]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}.

By incorporating (
5.254
), (
5.266
), (
5.276
) to (
5.253
), we arrive at

(5.278)

\displaystyle A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]

\displaystyle\lesssim X\cdot TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+(X+D)\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]+D^{2}X+D^{3}.

Summarizing the area estimates above, we have proven the following:

(1)

(
5.103
):

(5.279)

\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}]\lesssim\;D\left(1+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]\right)+A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}],

(2)

(
5.125
):

(5.280)

\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}]\lesssim\;TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}|_{\Gamma}]+D\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)+A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}],

(3)

(
5.133
)

(5.281)

TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}|_{\Gamma}]\lesssim D\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]\right)+A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}],

(4)

(
5.175
):

(5.282)

\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\log\lambda]\lesssim D^{2}+DX+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu],

(5)

(
5.184
):

(5.283)

\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\log|\nu|]\lesssim D^{2}+DY+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\log\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda],

(6)

(
5.186
):

(5.284)

TV_{(0,1)}[\log(\lambda|_{\Gamma})]\lesssim D^{2}+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu],

(7)

(
5.193
):

(5.285)

A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]\lesssim DY\left(1+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]\right)+D^{2}+(D+Y)\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}],

(8)

(
5.200
):

(5.286)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]\lesssim

\displaystyle X\cdot TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}|_{\Gamma}]+DX\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)

\displaystyle+D^{2}+(D+X)\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}],

(9)

(
5.242
):

(5.287)

A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim(D+Y)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+D^{2}\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]+D^{2}Y+D^{3},

(10)

(
5.278
):

(5.288)

A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim X\cdot TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+(X+D)\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]+D^{2}X+D^{3}.

Note that we have used
A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]=A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]
and
A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger}]=A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]
to simplify and to rewrite (
5.285
) - (
5.288
).

Recall that by our hypothesis, we have that (
5.17
) holds. Plugging (1) to (6) into (7) to (10), keeping the dominant terms, and employing
TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}|_{\Gamma}]=TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}|_{\Gamma}]
, we obtain

(7’):

(5.289)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]

\displaystyle\lesssim DY\left(1+D^{2}+DX+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]\right)+D^{2}+(D+Y)\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}+DZ\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+(D+Z)A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}].

(8’):

(5.290)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]\lesssim

\displaystyle X\left(D\left(1+TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\lambda]\right)+A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]\right)+DX

\displaystyle+DX\left(TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]\right)+DX\left(\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)+D^{2}+(D+X)\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}],

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle DX\left(TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\lambda|_{\Gamma}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\lambda]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]\right)

\displaystyle+X\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]+(D+X)\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]+DX+D^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle DX\left(A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]\right)+X\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]+(D+X)\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]+DX+D^{2}.

For
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,1)
being sufficiently small, inequality (8’) implies

(5.291)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]\lesssim

\displaystyle DZ\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+Z\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]+(D+Z)\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]+DZ+D^{2}.

To further simplify (9’) and (10’), we consider the following simplifications to (9) and (10)

(5.292)

A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+D^{2}\cdot A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]+D^{2}Z+D^{3}

and

(5.293)

A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim Z\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+(D+Z)\cdot A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]+D^{2}Z+D^{3}.

Plugging (
5.293
) into (
5.292
), we get

(5.294)

A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\lambda]+D^{2}(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\nu]+D^{4}\cdot A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]+D^{2}Z+D^{3}.

For
\varepsilon_{0}
sufficiently small, we thus obtain

(9’)

:

(5.295)

A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}Z+D^{3}.

We then plug (
5.295
) to (
5.293
) and obtain

(10’)

:

(5.296)

A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+(D+Z)A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]+D^{2}Z+D^{3}.

Substituting (9’) and (10’) into (7’) and (8’), we then derive

(5.297)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}+(D+Z)^{2}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+(D+Z)^{2}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]

and

(5.298)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}+(D+Z)^{2}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]+(D+Z)^{2}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda].

With a sufficiently small
\varepsilon_{0}
, (
5.297
) and (
5.298
) imply

(5.299)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}+(D+Z)^{2}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]

and

(5.300)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}+(D+Z)^{2}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda].

By employing (
5.298
) into (
5.297
), we thus obtain

(5.301)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}+(D+Z)^{4}A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda].

By shrinking
\varepsilon_{0}
if necessary, inequality (
5.301
) gives

(5.302)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}/\lambda]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}.

Together with (
5.298
), this implies that

(5.303)

\displaystyle A[\tilde{\phi}^{\dagger},\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}/\nu]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}.

Substituting (
5.302
) and (
5.303
) back to (
5.295
) and (
5.296
), we hence get

(5.304)

A[\lambda,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim DZ^{2}+D^{2}Z+D^{3}\quad\text{ and }\quad A[\nu,\tilde{\phi}]\lesssim DZ^{2}+D^{2}Z+D^{3}.

Plugging (
5.302
), (
5.303
), (
5.304
) into (4), (5), (6), we thus prove

(5.305)

\displaystyle\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\log\lambda]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2},

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\log|\nu|]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2},

\displaystyle TV_{(0,1)}[\log(\lambda|_{\Gamma})]

\displaystyle\lesssim DZ+D^{2}.

By applying all the above estimates to (1), (2), (3), we now conclude

(5.306)

\displaystyle\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}]\lesssim D,\quad\quad\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}]\lesssim D,\quad\text{ and }\quad TV_{(0,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}|_{\Gamma}]\lesssim D.

In view of Lemma
5.2
and (
5.11
), we observe that

(5.307)

\displaystyle X

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}\left[\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}}{\lambda}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\lambda]+\sup_{u\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{\{u\}\times(u,v_{0})}[\tilde{\mathfrak{a}}]\lesssim D.

A similar argument yields

(5.308)

\displaystyle Y

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\tilde{\raisebox{0.0pt}[0.85pt]{$\tilde{\mathfrak{b}}$}}]+\sup_{v\in(0,v_{0})}TV_{(0,v)\times\{v\}}[\nu]+D\lesssim D.

Henceforth, by employing (
5.307
) and (
5.308
), we arrive at

(5.309)

Z\lesssim D.

Substituting (
5.309
) back into (
5.302
), (
5.303
), (
5.304
), (
5.305
), we thus verify that (
5.19
) and (
5.20
) holds.

∎

6.
C^{1}
Extension Theorems

We now establish the
C^{1}
extension theorem in this section. We begin by defining

(6.1)

\alpha^{\prime}:=\frac{1}{\partial_{v}r}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\partial_{v}(r\phi)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)=\frac{\partial_{v}\alpha}{\partial_{v}r}.

Recall that the
C^{1}
-regularity of the solution to the system is equivalent to the boundedness of the term
|\alpha^{\prime}|
defined in (
6.1
). Furthermore, for the charged scalar field system, the
Doppler exponent

\gamma
is defined in (
1.33
) and given by

\gamma(u,v):=\int_{0}^{u}\frac{-\partial_{u}r}{r}\frac{\mu-Q^{2}/r^{2}}{1-\mu}(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

Our results for this section are summarized in the two theorems below.

Theorem 6.1
.

(
C^{1}
Extension Theorem with Doppler Exponent
\gamma
.) Suppose that we have
C^{1}
initial data
\alpha
along
C_{0}^{+}
. Let
v_{*}>0
and assume for every
\hat{v}\in(0,v_{*})
, a
C^{1}
solution exists on
\mathcal{D}(0,\hat{v})
. If we have

(6.2)

\sup_{\mathcal{D}(0,v_{*})}\gamma<+\infty,

then a
C^{1}
solution can be extended to
\mathcal{D}(0,\tilde{v})
with
\tilde{v}>v_{*}
.

As mentioned in the introduction, analogous to
[
13
]
, the above theorem implies that the inability to extend the solution past some
v_{*}>0
necessitates the “finite-time” blowup of the Doppler exponent, corresponding to an integration of some physical quantity.

A related but independent
C^{1}
extension theorem with mass ratio for the charged system, motivated by
[
19
]
, is given as follows.

Theorem 6.2
.

(
C^{1}
Extension Theorem with Mass Ratio
\mu
.) Suppose that we have
C^{1}
initial data
\alpha
along
C_{0}^{+}
. Let
v_{*}>0
and assume for every
\hat{v}\in(0,v_{*})
, a
C^{1}
solution exists on
\mathcal{D}(0,\hat{v})
. Then, there exists a constant
\varepsilon^{\prime}>0
, such that if we have

(6.3)

\lim_{u\rightarrow v_{*}}\sup_{\mathcal{D}(u,v_{*})}\mu<\varepsilon^{\prime},

then a
C^{1}
solution can be extended to
\mathcal{D}(0,\tilde{v})
for some
\tilde{v}>v_{*}
.

In this section, we will first prove Theorem
6.1
, followed by Theorem
6.2
. For both theorems, we will obtain the corresponding extension theorems via two steps. In the first step, under the assumptions in Theorem
6.1
or Theorem
6.2
, we show that the solution can be extended as a
C^{1}
solution up to
\mathcal{D}(0,v_{*}).
In the second step, we then extend such a
C^{1}
solution to
\mathcal{D}(0,\tilde{v})
with some
\tilde{v}>v_{*}
, which completes the proofs.

\mathcal{O}
\Gamma
C_{0}^{+}
\mathcal{D}(0,\hat{v})
\hat{v}
v_{*}
\tilde{v}
(u,u)
(v_{*},v_{*})
\mathcal{D}(u,v_{*})

Figure 7.
Penrose diagram representing the relative positions of
\hat{v},v_{*},\tilde{v}
and the regions
\mathcal{D}(0,\hat{v})
and
\mathcal{D}(u,v_{*})
.

Before we begin with the proof, we list several computations that are commonly used in both proofs.

Lemma 6.3
.

For any
v_{*}>0
and
u_{1}\in(0,v_{*})
, assume that a
C^{1}
solution exists in
\mathcal{D}(0,v_{*})\setminus\mathcal{D}(u_{1},v_{*})
. For each
(u,v)\in\mathcal{D}(0,v_{*})\setminus\mathcal{D}(u_{1},v_{*})
, we have

(6.4)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\left(\alpha^{\prime}e^{\int^{u}_{u_{0}}\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=e^{\int^{u}_{u_{0}}\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}A_{u}(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}}\sum_{i=1}^{11}K_{i}(u,v)

with

(6.5)

\displaystyle K_{1}(u,v)

\displaystyle=-\frac{8\pi\mathfrak{e}Q\left(\im(\phi^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\phi)\right)(\partial_{v}\phi)(\partial_{u}r)}{(\partial_{v}r)^{2}(1-\mu)},

\displaystyle K_{2}(u,v)=\frac{4\pi Q^{2}|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}(\partial_{v}\phi)(\partial_{u}r)}{r(\partial_{v}r)^{3}(1-\mu)},

\displaystyle K_{3}(u,v)

\displaystyle=-\frac{4\pi r|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}(\partial_{v}\phi)(\partial_{u}r)}{(\partial_{v}r)^{3}(1-\mu)},

\displaystyle K_{4}(u,v)=-\frac{2\partial_{u}r\mu}{r(1-\mu)}\alpha^{\prime},

\displaystyle K_{5}(u,v)

\displaystyle=\frac{2Q^{2}\partial_{u}r}{r^{3}(1-\mu)}\alpha^{\prime},

\displaystyle K_{6}(u,v)=\frac{5Q^{2}\partial_{u}r\partial_{v}\phi}{r^{3}\partial_{v}r(1-\mu)},

\displaystyle K_{7}(u,v)

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\partial_{u}r\partial_{v}\phi\mu}{r\partial_{v}r(1-\mu)},

\displaystyle K_{8}(u,v)=-\frac{4\pi\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}|\partial_{v}\phi|^{2}Q\phi\partial_{u}r}{(1-\mu)(\partial_{v}r)^{2}},

\displaystyle K_{9}(u,v)

\displaystyle=\frac{4\mathrm{i}\pi\mathfrak{e}^{2}\phi\im(\phi^{\dagger}\partial_{v}\phi)r\partial_{u}r}{\partial_{v}r(1-\mu)},

\displaystyle K_{10}(u,v)=-\frac{3\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}Q\partial_{u}r\partial_{v}\phi}{r(1-\mu)(\partial_{v}r)},

\displaystyle K_{11}(u,v)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mathrm{i}\mathfrak{e}Q\phi\partial_{u}r}{r^{2}(1-\mu)}.

Furthermore, if
\gamma(u,v)\leq G
and
\frac{1}{1-\mu(u,v)}\leq H
uniformly in
(u,v)\in\mathcal{D}(0,v_{*})\setminus\mathcal{D}(u_{1},v_{*})
for some
G,H>0
, then for each of these
(u,v)
and any
\chi,\iota\in(0,1)
, we have the following estimates:

(6.6)

\displaystyle|Q|(u,v)

\displaystyle\leq C_{6}^{\prime}(v_{*})\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,v)r(u,v),

\displaystyle|Q\phi|(u,v)

\displaystyle\leq C_{7}^{\prime\prime}(v_{*})\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,v)r^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{\chi}{2}}(u,v)\leq C_{7}^{\prime}(v_{*})\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,v)r(u,v),

\displaystyle|\phi|^{2}(u,v)

\displaystyle\leq C_{8}^{\prime}(v_{*})r^{-\frac{3}{2}}(u,v),

with

(6.7)

C_{6}^{\prime}(v_{*};\chi):=C_{6}(v_{*};\chi)r^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\chi}{2}}(0,v_{*}),

(6.8)

C_{7}^{\prime}(v_{*};\chi):=C_{7}^{\prime\prime}(v_{*};\chi)r^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\chi}{2}}(0,v_{*}),\quad C_{7}^{\prime\prime}(v_{*};\chi):=C_{7}(v_{*};\chi)\frac{e^{\frac{1}{2}G}}{(1-\mu_{*})^{\frac{1}{2}}},

(6.9)

C_{8}^{\prime}(v_{*};\iota):=\frac{H}{2\pi}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(0,v_{*})+2C_{8}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right),

and

(6.10)

C_{6}(v_{*};\chi):=\frac{2\pi\mathfrak{e}}{1-\mu^{*}}\left(\frac{\overline{P}(0,v_{*})}{\sqrt{4\pi}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{16\pi^{2}\chi\mathfrak{e}^{2}}}\right)r^{\frac{\chi}{2}}(0,v_{*})v_{*}^{\frac{1}{2}},

(6.11)

C_{7}(v_{*};\chi)=\left(8\pi\mathfrak{e}^{2}C_{3}(0,v_{*};\chi,1,\chi)+\frac{C_{6}^{2}}{2(2-\chi)}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

(6.12)

C_{8}(\iota):=\overline{P}(0,v_{*})r(0,v_{*})^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\iota}{2}}+\frac{v_{*}^{\frac{1}{2}}r(0,v_{*})^{\frac{\iota}{2}}}{(4\pi e^{2}\iota(1-\iota))^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{\bar{B}(0,v_{*})}{(1-\overline{\mu_{*}})(0,v_{*})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

On the other hand, if
\mu(u,v)\leq\varepsilon
for some
\varepsilon<1
for each
(u,v)\in\mathcal{D}(0,v_{*})\setminus\mathcal{D}(u_{1},v_{*})
, we can deduce

(6.13)

\displaystyle|Q|(u,v)

\displaystyle\leq C_{6}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}r^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{\chi}{2}}(u,v),

\displaystyle|Q\phi|(u,v)

\displaystyle\leq C_{7}\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1-\varepsilon)^{\frac{1}{2}}}r^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{\chi}{2}}(u,v),

\displaystyle|\phi|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\varepsilon}+2C_{8}^{2}r^{-1-\iota}(u,v).

Remark 12
.

Note that
|Q|,|Q\phi|,|\phi|^{2}
controlled in Lemma
6.3
represent additional terms unique to our charged system. The methodologies employed to derive bounds for these terms are therefore new.

Proof of Lemma
6.3
.
Starting from the definition of
\alpha^{\prime}
in (
6.1
), we compute

(6.14)

\displaystyle\partial_{u}\alpha^{\prime}=\partial_{u}\left(\frac{1}{\partial_{v}r}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\partial_{v}(r\phi)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\partial_{u}\partial_{v}r}{(\partial_{v}r)^{2}}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\partial_{v}(r\phi)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)+\frac{1}{\partial_{v}r}\partial_{u}\partial_{v}\left(\frac{\partial_{v}(r\phi)}{\partial_{v}r}\right)
